Objective:
```
def lse_obj_grad(z, beta, maybe offset): # returns f, grad_z
   a = softmax(z)
   b = autocorr(a)
   m = b.max()
   e = beta*(b-m)
   p = exp(e); p /= p.sum()
   f = m + (1/beta)*log(p.sum()) # = (1/beta)logsumexp
   # grad b wrt a = 2*valid_correlate(p,a)
   ga = 2*valid_correlate(p,a)
   # grad wrt z: a*(ga - dot(a, ga))
   grad = a * (ga - np.dot(a, ga))
   # optional scale to prevent large
   return f, grad
```

def coarse_search(best_a, best_score, time_limit):
   m_list = [64, 128, 256, 512]
   for m in m_list:
      for starts in [rng.random(m), interpolation of best, onset etc]:
          a = norm_sum1(start)
          # optimize using L-BFGS or gradient with β up to maybe 200 (coarse)
          # track best_m
          # after coarse, interpolate to current n and update best
```

Let's detail `lse_objective(z, beta)`:
```
def lse_objective(z, beta):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    zc = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
    zm = zc - zc.max()
    a = np.exp(zm)
    a_sum = a.sum()
    if a_sum <=0: a = np.ones_like(z)/len(z)
    else: a = a/a_sum
    b = fast_autocorr(a)
    # maybe trim rounding: b[:2n-1]
    M = b.max()
    e = beta*(b - M)
    # if beta large, e can under/overflow? beta up to 2000, b diff up to 1e-3 => 2; ok.
    p = np.exp(e)
    p_sum = p.sum()
    if p_sum <=0 or not finite: p = uniform top?; p_sum=...
    f = M + np.log(p_sum)/beta
    ga = 2.0 * valid_corr(p, a)
    grad = a * (ga - np.dot(a, ga))
    # maybe scale grad by 1e2? L-BFGS expects f scale? f ~100? Actually b~1e-4, f ~1e-4. Grad ~? a*ga ~ (1/n)*? maybe 1e-5? The Hessian scale high? L-BFGS okay but small numbers. Could multiply objective and grad by 1e6 to improve condition. Let scale = 1e6. f*scale, grad*scale.
    return f*scale, grad*scale
```
Scaling by 1e6 makes f ~100, grad ~10. Good. Use `SCALE = 100000`.

But L-BFGS with objective scale: The line search uses f differences; scaling okay. Need beta as float in args.

def gradient_polish(a0, time_limit, beta_start, beta_end):
  a = norm_sum1(a0)
  b,m=...
  best=a,best_m=m
  beta=beta_start
  eta=0.5
  v=np.zeros(n)
  mom=0.9
  it=0
  while time:
    p=softmax beta*(b-m)
    ga=2*valid_corr(p,a)
    g=ga - np.dot(a,ga)  # tangent mean? maybe (ga*1).mean? To keep sum zero subtract mean? Since sum constraint, use ga - ga.mean()? But a nonuniform; L-BFGS used dot(a,ga). Which tangent correct? The function is invariant under adding constant to ga? For simplex projection, subtracting any constant equivalent. For z gradient, dot(a,ga). For projection, subtract mean? The tangent to sum constraint is orthogonal to 1, so subtract mean (ga - ga.mean()) not dot(a,ga). But if using gradient of f on unconstrained extended, the projection after step handles constant, so not necessary. For heavy-ball on a, using centered gradient maybe.
    v = mom*v - eta*ga
    x = simplex_project(a+v)
    # line search over alphas for direction d = x-a? Or use z? 
```

Let's refine LP direction correctly.
Given a, active top K by b (maybe include all if K≤n). Solve:
min t
s.t. 2 X_k - 2 b_k ≤ t for k active
sum g=1, g≥0, t free.
This is equivalent row: 2 C_k g - t ≤ 2 b_k.
If solve, direction g. This minimizes maximum first-order change over active lags. For a full Frank-Wolfe for max over active, if we ignore constraints not active, direction may increase inactive lags. But line search will catch and next active update top includes new peaks. Good.

Could add a regularization to keep g not too far: minimize t + β_reg ||gHPD||? Not linear. Could bound by using convex combination with a after line search.

Implement LP solver function:
```
def solve_lp_direction(a, b, active_indices, verbose=False):
    K = len(active_indices)
    if K==0: return None
    n=len(a)
    # optional sort active by b descending already
    # Build sparse rows
    row_list=[]; col_list=[]; data_list=[]
    for row,k in enumerate(active_indices):
       k=int(k)
       start=max(0, k-(n-1)); end=min(n-1,k)
       if start>end: continue
       cols=np.arange(start,end+1)
       vals=2.0 * a[k-cols]
       row_list.append(np.full(len(cols), row))
       col_list.append(cols)
       data_list.append(vals)
       # t column
       row_list.append(np.array([row])); col_list.append(np.array([n])); data_list.append(np.array([-1.0]))
    if any empty: return None
    A_ub = csr_matrix(vstack(data), shape=(K,n+1))
    b_ub = 2.0 * np.array(b[active_indices])
    A_eq = csr_matrix(([1.0], (n, np.arange(n))), shape=(1,n+1))
    c = zeros(n+1); c[n]=1
    res = linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1], bounds=[(0,None)]*n + [(-10,10)], method='highs', options={'presolve': True, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance': 1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9})
    if res.status !=0: return None
    g = np.maximum(res.x[:n],0)
    gs=g.sum(); if gs<... return None
    return g/gs
```
But if K rows have no nonzeros? skip. Need track rows with no valid? k 0..2n-2 always valid.
Potential problem: A_ub with duplicate entries? For each row, t column separate; no duplicates. Good.
`linprog` bounds list length n+1; for n=8192 list creation okay. But `((0, None),)*(n)` memory okay. Use `bounds=[(0, None)]*n + [(-5.0,5.0)]`.

Could solve be too slow? We can cache? a changes. Active top changes. We'll call limited times.

def softmax_gradient_polish(a0, time_limit, beta):
  a=norm; z=log(a)
  best...

  lr=0.5
  mu=0.9

    b=autocorr(a)
    M=b.max(); p=exp(beta*(b-M)); p/=sum
    ga=2*valid_corr(p,a)
    grad = a*(ga - dot(a,ga))
    # clip grad norm
    gnorm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
    if gnorm>1e6: grad*=1e6/gnorm
    # v = mu*v + grad? for heavy-ball on adaptive?

Adam implementation:
```
def adam_soft_gradient(a0, time_limit, beta_start, beta_end):
   a=norm; z=log(a)
   m=np.zeros(n); v=np.zeros(n); t=0
   beta=beta_start; lr=1.0? For z, grad scale ~? a*ga ~ (1/n)*? maybe 1e-5; Adam normalizes, so lr can be ~1e-3 to 0.1.
   best_m=...
   while time:
      b=autocorr(a); M=b.max(); p=softmax(beta*(b-M)); ga=2*valid_corr(p,a); grad=a*(ga-dot(a,ga))
      # For small a, grad small; okay.
      t+=1; beta=min(beta_end,beta*1.002)
      beta? if change, recompute?
      # normalize grad to unit? Use per-coordinate Adam.
      m=0.9*m+0.1*grad
      v=0.999*v+0.001*grad*grad
      z = z - lr * m/(sqrt(v)+1e-8)
      z = clip(z,-50,50); a=softmax(z)
      m_new=autocorr(a).max()
      if m_new < best_m: best=...
      else if many no_improve: reduce lr or reset to best with perturbation
```
But computing autocorr twice if a & z update? We need b for gradient and m_new after update; one extra. Could be okay.

We can use many iterations. Let's design `multiplicative_peak_flattening`:
```
def multiplicative_update(a, b, beta, eta_candidates):
   M=b.max()
   p=exp(beta*(b-M)); p/=sum
   pressure = valid_corr(p,a)  # unnormalized, nonnegative
   # subtract? If all pressure large, exp negative; normalize.
   # To avoid global scale, use pressure centered? Multiplicative with raw pressure reduces all; normalization handles. But if pressure roughly constant, no shape change.
   # Use relative: r = pressure / (self?); a_new = a * exp(-eta*(pressure - small_mean))? Since sum normalization, constant factor irrelevant. So subtract median or mean of pressure for shape.
   base = np.median(pressure) (or mean)
   shape = (pressure - base) / (np.std(pressure)+eps)
   # clip shape to [-5,5] to avoid huge
   for eta in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.4,0.8]:
       a_new = a * exp(-eta*shape)
       normalize; m=...
       if m<best: accept, break
```
But if pressure has negative? pressure nonnegative; shape negative for low pressure => increases those positions. Good. This pushes mass from high-pressure positions to low-pressure positions. It's a coordinate multiplicative Newton. Since pressure is linear in a with p, it may find balance where pressure proportional to something. In fact, multiplicative update for minimizing weighted sum of products tends to equalize pressure on support. This is similar to "artificial intelligence balancing".

This could be very effective and cheap. Let's analyze: We want minimize max b. p weights high peaks. Pressure_i = ∑ p_k a_{k-i}; high pressure means variable participates in high coefficients. Reducing high pressure variables lowers peaks. Alternating p updates adapt. This is like Bregman projection for convex if q convex; but works here.

Step size: Need avoid zeros. Use shape centered and clipped. We can do backtracking on actual max. Since small eta many iterations.

We can combine with top-k p or full softmax. Full p over all lags: pressure is weighted average of shifted a. For b flat, p uniform, pressure uniform? If p uniform, pressure_i = (1/L)∑ a_j (depending on edge count) nearly constant, small shape. So update stalls when b near flat except boundaries; need β high to emphasize peaks. Start β=50-500, increase.

This multiplicative update is different from previous subgradient and likely efficient. Let's prioritize.

   # Use logarithmic update: a_i <- a_i / (1 + c * r_i) with r_i = pressure_i / (mean? or a_i?)

Let's design `multiplicative_loop(a0, time_limit, beta_start=20, beta_end=2000)`:
- Return best_a, best_m.
- Use deterministic RNG for perturb.
- To avoid too many beta underflow, compute `M = b.max(); shifted = beta*(b-M); max_shift=0; min_shift = min shifted; if min_shift < -700, p=zero; set p at top 100 uniformly? - `pressure = valid_corr(p,a)`. If p all equal? If β very low and b not flat, p slight.

- Clip z/scale to [-4,4]. Maybe use `tanh(z/scale)` for smooth: `shape = np.tanh(z/scale)`. This keeps update bounded. Good.
- Candidate eta: Use global adaptive `eta`. We can evaluate two: eta and eta*2? Let's use one adaptive to save.
Pseudo:
```
eta = 0.1
no_improve=0; beta=beta_start; perturb_level=0
while time<limit:
   if no_improve>0 and no_improve%100==0:
       # random multiplicative noise from best
       a = best * exp(rng.normal(0, 0.01*level)); normalize
       b=...
   M=b.max(); p=...
   pressure=valid_corr(p,a)
   if pressure.max()-pressure.min() < 1e-18: # break/perturb
   q1=np.percentile(pressure,25); q3=np.percentile(pressure,75); scale=max(q3-q1,1e-18)
   z=(pressure - (q1+q3)/2)/scale
   z=np.tanh(z) # in [-1,1]
   if noise: z += rng.normal(0, 0.01*level,n)
   a_new = a * np.exp(-eta*z)
   a_new = np.clip(a_new,0,None); normalize
   b_new=fast_autocorr(a_new); m_new=b_new.max()
   # Update current rule
   if m_new < best_m:
       best_m=m_new; best=a_new.copy(); a=a_new; b=b_new; no_improve=0; eta=min(eta*1.1, 1.5)
   elif m_new < m: # improved current (if a not best)
       a=a_new; b=b_new; m=m_new; no_improve=0; eta=min(eta*1.05,1.5)
   else:
       no_improve+=1
       eta=max(eta*0.75, 0.005)
       # if eta too small or no_improve > 50, perturb current from best with increasing noise and reset eta
       if no_improve > 50 or eta < 0.01:
           noise = 0.005 * min(5, no_improve/50)
           a = best * np.exp(rng.normal(0, noise, n))
           a=norm_sum1(a)
           b=fast_autocorr(a); m=b.max()
           eta=0.05
   # beta schedule: if improved, beta*=1.005 up to end; if no_improve high, reduce beta? Actually higher beta more active peaks; maybe increase slowly always.
   if t % 50 ==0: beta=min(beta_end,beta*1.02)
```
But if a always best on no_improve (we don't set a to nonbest), then `m_new<m` never true except if a is nonbest after perturb. Function:
```
def top_pressure(b, a, K, beta):
   idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]  # not sorted maybe
   vals = b[idx]
   # weights softmax beta*(vals - vals.max())
   e=np.exp(beta*(vals-vals.max())); w=e/e.sum()
   # sort descending for vector maybe not needed
   p=np.zeros(len?); 
   pressure=zeros(n)
   for val,k in zip(w,idx): 
       k=int(k); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k); # length
       partner = k - np.arange(s,e+1)
       pressure[s:e+1] += val * a[partner]
   return pressure
```

Let's design `multiplicative_flatten` in more detail, correct for top pressure.

```
def multiplicative_flatten(a0, time_limit, beta_start=10.0, beta_end=500.0, K_start=32, K_end=256, verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n=len(a)
    b = fast_autocorr(a)
    m = b.max()
    best_a = a.copy(); best_m = m
    cur_m = m
    rng = np.random.default_rng(1234)
    t0=time.monotonic(); beta=beta_start; eta=0.08; no=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit:
        it+=1
        K = int(min(K_end, K_start + it*0.05)) # grows
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:] if K < len(b) else np.arange(len(b))
        vals = b[idx]
        # weights: softmax beta*(vals - max)
        vv = vals - vals.max()
        e = np.exp(beta*vv)
        esum=e.sum()
        if esum <= 0 or not finite:
            w = np.ones(K)/K
        else: w=e/esum
        pressure = np.zeros(n)
        for wk,kp in zip(w, idx):
            if wk <= 0: continue
            kp=int(kp)
            s=max(0, kp-n+1); epos=min(n-1,kp)
            # partners kp - (s..epos)
            # use slice: a[kp-s : kp-epos-1 : -1]? Let's test.
            if s <= epos:
                # partner indices from kp-s down to kp-epos
                pressure[s:epos+1] += wk * a[kp-s : kp-epos-1 : -1]
                # if kp-epos-1 negative, Python stop None? Need handle.
        # If slice invalid, use index array safer:
```
Need verify slice `a[start : stop : -1]`. For reverse slice, start is beginning (larger), stop is one before ending (smaller, exclusive). We want indices `kp-s, kp-s-1, ..., kp-epos`. So start = kp-s, stop = kp-epos - 1. If kp-epos =0, stop=-1 => Python with step -1 and stop=-1 means stop index -1 (last)?? For negative stop with negative step, it's interpreted; tricky. Use `a[kp-s : kp-epos-1 : -1]`; if kp-epos-1 = -1, it stops before index -1 (i.e., includes 0). Example kp-s=5, kp-epos=0 => reverse sequence 5,4,3,2,1,0. Slice a[5:-1:-1] includes 5..0? In Python, a[5:-1:-1] stops before -1 which is index len-1, so includes 0? Let's test mentally: len=6, a[5:-1:-1] gives indices 5,4,3,2,1,0? I think -1 exclusive means stop at index len-1? For negative step, stop is lower bound; -1 is len-1, so indices > -1? Actually for a[5:-1:-1], it starts 5, decreases until index 0? It includes 0 because -1 maps to index 5? Wait Python: `range(5, -1, -1)` includes 5..0. Slice stop -1 with negative step corresponds to index -1 absolute len-1? For len 6, -1=5, range(5,5,-1) empty? But slices handle differently. Better avoid. Use index array.
```

            pressure[s:epos+1] += wk * a[kp - pos]
```

        p_min = pressure.min(); p_max=pressure.max()

            last_print...

LP direct pressure with top weights also can be thought of solving active equalization. The multiplicative algorithm approximates solving H_p a constant. LP solves different condition (min max first-order). Both useful.

Let's design `active_lp_loop`:
```
def active_lp_loop(a0, time_limit, K=150, max_iters=200):
   a = norm_sum1(a0)
   b=fast_autocorr(a); best_a=a; best_m=b.max()
   cur_m=best_m
   t0=...; it=0; no=0; rng=...
   while time<limit and it<max_iters:
      it+=1
      active = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
      # sort maybe by b
      active = active[np.argsort(b[active])[::-1]]
      g = solve_lp_direction(a, b, active)
      if g is None:
         # perturb
         ...
         continue
      # line search
      best_alpha=0; best_trial=a; best_trial_m=cur_m
      cur_b=b; cur_m=cur_b.max()
      for al in [0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8,1.0, 0.15,0.45]:
          trial=(1-al)*a + al*g
          trial=np.maximum(trial,0)
          s=trial.sum(); if s<... continue
          trial/=s
          m_trial=fast_autocorr(trial).max()
          if m_trial < best_trial_m:
             best_trial_m=m_trial; best_alpha=al; best_trial=trial
      if best_alpha>0:
         a=best_trial; cur_m=best_trial_m; b=fast_autocorr(a)
         if cur_m < best_m: best_a=a.copy(); best_m=cur_m; no=0
         else: no+=1
      else: no++
      if no%30==0:
         noise=0.005; a=norm_sum1(best_a*exp(rng.normal(0,noise,n))); cur_m=fast_autocorr(a).max()
         no=0
      print...
   return best_a,best_m
```
In line search, `fast_autocorr(a)` repeated; if best_alpha found, we need b for next iteration. We can store. If best_alpha=0, keep b.

Potential performance: Each iteration LP solve + 9 autocorrs. If LP 0.5s and autocorrs 9*0.3ms, total ~0.5s; 200 iters 100s. K=150 sparse. Good.

Could reduce line search alphas to 5.

def fast_autocorr(a):

Let's detail helper functions:

```
def norm_sum1(a):
    a=np.maximum(np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64),0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s<1e-300: return np.full_like(a,1.0/a.size)
    return a/s
```

```
def interpolate_sequence(f,new_n):
    f=norm_sum1(f)
    if len(f)==new_n: return f
    old_n=len(f)
    # use linear interpolation on cell centers to preserve sum? Use x old = (i+0.5)/old_n, new=(j+0.5)/new_n.
    x_old=np.linspace(0,1,old_n, endpoint=False?) 
```

```
def top_pressure(a, b, K, beta):
    n=len(a); L=2*n-1; K=min(K,L)
    idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
    vals=b[idx]
    vmax=vals.max()
    e=np.exp(beta*(vals-vmax))
    es=e.sum()
    if es <=0 or not np.isfinite(es):
        w=np.full(K,1.0/K)
    else: w=e/es
    pressure=np.zeros(n)
    for j in range(K):
       wj=w[j]
       if wj <= 1e-18: continue
       k=int(idx[j])
       s=max(0, k - n + 1); epos=min(n-1,k)
       if s > epos: continue
       # positions np.arange(s, epos+1)
       # To avoid allocation, maybe use while loop? But vector.
       pos = np.arange(s, epos+1)
       pressure[s:epos+1] += wj * a[k - pos]
    return pressure
```
This allocates pos each. We can precompute `arange_cache` for each possible s,e? Too many. But okay. Could use slice with reversed view safely:

else:

pressure[s:epos+1] += wj * part
```

def full_softmax_weights(b, beta):
    M=b.max()
    e=np.exp(beta*(b-M))
    s=e.sum()
    if s <=0 or not finite:
       p=np.zeros_like(b); p[np.argsort(b)[-min(50,len(b)):]]=1/...
    else: p=e/s
    return p
```

`smooth_lbfgs`:
```
def smooth_lbfgs(a0, time_limit, beta_stages=(50,150,400), verbose=True):
    a=norm_sum1(a0); best_a=a; best_m=fast_autocorr(a).max()
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for beta in beta_stages:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
       z = z - z.mean()  # center
       remaining=time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0)
       if remaining < 30: break
       maxiter = int(min(120, max(20, remaining/3)))
       try:
          res = optimize.minimize(lse_objective, z, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                                  options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxiter*30, 'maxls':20, 'disp': False, 'maxcor': 10})
       except Exception:
          continue
       if res.x is not None:
          a_new = softmax(res.x)
          b=fast_autocorr(a_new); m=b.max()
          if m < best_m:
             a_new; best...
          # also set current a to new if m < current_m? Track current_m
       else: a=best_a
       print
    return best_a,best_m
```
Need import `scipy.optimize as optimize` top-level? We can `import scipy.optimize as sciopt`. No issue.

def softmax(z):
   z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
   z=z-z.max()
   e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s==0: return ones/n; return e/s
```

def solve_lp_direction(a,b,active):
    n=len(a); K=len(active)
    if K==0: return None
    rows=[]; cols=[]; data=[]
    row_arrays? Use Python lists; for 200 rows, each length ~5000, total 1M appends; okay but maybe slow. Use preallocation? Could be okay.
    # We can collect per row arrays and concatenate.
    row_chunks=[]; col_chunks=[]; data_chunks=[]
    for r,k in enumerate(active):
       k=int(k); s=max(0,k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
       # partners slice: a[k-s : k-e-1 : -1] or a[k-s::-1]
       if k-e == 0: part=a[k-s::-1]
       else: part=a[k-s:k-e-1:-1]
       if part.size:
          col_chunks.append(np.arange(s, e+1))
          data_chunks.append(2.0*part)
          row_chunks.append(np.full(part.size, r, dtype=np.int32))
       # t column
       col_chunks.append(np.array([n], dtype=np.int32))
       data_chunks.append(np.array([-1.0]))
       row_chunks.append(np.array([r], dtype=np.int32))
    if not data_chunks: return None
    A=sp.csr_matrix((np.concatenate(data_chunks), (np.concatenate(row_chunks), np.concatenate(col_chunks))), shape=(K, n+1))
    b_ub = 2.0 * np.asarray(b[active], dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq = sp.csr_matrix(([1.0], (n, np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32))), shape=(1,n+1))
    c = np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
    bounds = [(0.0, None)]*n + [(-1.0, 10.0)]
    res = optimize.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
```

Now let's detail each function.

`norm_sum1`:
```
def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim !=1:
       a=a.ravel()
    a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
    s=a.sum()
    if s < 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
       return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/max(1,a.size)
    return a/s
```

`fast_autocorr`:
```
def fast_autocorr(a):
    a=np.asarray(a,dtype=np.float64)
    n=a.size
    size=2*n-1
    N=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    F=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    b=np.fft.irfft(F*F,N)[:size]
    # remove tiny imag? real
    # clip negative small
    b[np.abs(b)<1e-300]=0.0
    return b
```
`F*F` not conjugate; convolution of a with a, not autocorrelation. Correct. Since a real, no conjugate needed for polynomial square. For np.convolve(a,a), DFT = A*A. If used conjugate, it would be autocorrelation (a with reverse). So do not conjugate. Good.

Wait, linear convolution of a with itself using FFT: `irfft(fft(a)*fft(a))` gives cyclic convolution length N. With N ≥ 2n-1, linear. Correct. No conjugate.

`full_valid_corr(p,a)` for gradient:
```
def full_valid_corr(p, a):
    n=a.size; L=p.size
    if L < 2*n-1:
       p = np.pad(p, (0, (2*n-1)-L))
    size = L + n - 1
    N=1 << (size-1).bit_length()
    F=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    P=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    conv=np.fft.irfft(F*P, N)
    return conv[n-1:2*n-1]
```
Need p may be length exactly 2n-1. Good.

`softmax_logits`:
```
def softmax_logits(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,dtype=np.float64)
   z=np.clip(z,-50,50)
   z=z-z.max()
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<1e-300: return np.ones_like(z)/z.size
   return e/s
```

`lse_objective(z,beta)`:
```
def lse_objective(z, beta):
    a=softmax_logits(z)
    b=fast_autocorr(a)
    M=b.max()
    if not np.isfinite(M): return (1e30, np.zeros_like(z))
    ex=np.exp(beta*(b-M))
    s=ex.sum()
    if s < 1e-300 or not finite:
        # top 50 uniform
        p=np.zeros_like(b)
        K=min(50,len(b))
        p[np.argpartition(b,-K)[-K:]] =1.0/K
    else:
        p=ex/s
    grad_a=2.0*full_valid_corr(p,a)
    # Ensure real
    grad_z = a * (grad_a - np.dot(a, grad_a))
    f=(M + np.log(s)/beta)*OBJ_SCALE if s>0 else M*OBJ_SCALE
    if not finite f: f=M*OBJ_SCALE
    return f, grad_z*OBJ_SCALE
```
If s underflows, `np.log(s)` invalid. For top uniform fallback, logsum? If p uniform over top K, LSE not accurate. Use f=M*OBJ_SCALE. But gradient top uniform okay. We can set `lse_val = M + (np.log(s)/beta if s>0 else 0.0)`. Good.

Potential issue: `beta*(b-M)` if beta large and b-M negative large, underflow to 0; s maybe 1 if M repeated? fine. If b-M positive? M max, none. Good.

`smooth_lbfgs` uses `sciopt.minimize`. It should not use nested. It can define local variables but no nested functions.
```
def smooth_lbfgs(a0, time_limit, beta_stages=(50.0,150.0,400.0,900.0), verbose=True):
   a=norm_sum1(a0); b=fast_autocorr(a); best_m=b.max(); best=a.copy()
   t0=time.monotonic()
   for beta in beta_stages:
      elapsed=time.monotonic()-t0
      if elapsed > time_limit: break
      remaining=time_limit-elapsed
      if remaining < 25: break
      z=np.log(np.maximum(a,1e-12))
      z = z - z.mean()
      maxiter=max(15, min(120, int(remaining/4)))
      maxfun=max(200, maxiter*40)
      try:
         res=sciopt.minimize(lse_objective, z, args=(float(beta),), method='L-BFGS-B', jac=True,
                             options={'maxiter': maxiter, 'maxfun': maxfun, 'maxls': 20, 'disp': False, 'maxcor': 12})
      except Exception:
         continue
      if res is not None and res.x is not None:
         a_new=softmax_logits(res.x)
         b_new=fast_autocorr(a_new)
         m_new=b_new.max()
         if m_new < best_m:
             best_m=m_new; best=a_new.copy(); a=a_new
         else:
             # still update a to new if it improves over current? We may want to continue from new even if max not best but smooth lower. But could degrade max. Use new if m_new < current_m? current_m=fast_autocorr(a).max()
             current_m=fast_autocorr(a).max()
             if m_new < current_m:
                 a=a_new
             else:
                 a=best.copy()
      if verbose:
         print("  LBFGS beta=%g score=%.8f" % (beta, 2*len(best)*best_m))
   return best, best_m
```
If L-BFGS fails due time, may still spend. We control maxiter. It doesn't stop by real time; if one iteration slow, maxiter*40 evals may exceed time. Could reduce maxfun: `maxfun = min(max(100, maxiter*10), int(remaining/0.05?)` hard. Use modest: maxiter=40, maxfun=800 per stage. Four stages ~3200 evals. If 1ms each, 3s; if 10ms,32s. Remaining maybe. Good. Let's set maxiter = min(60, max(10, remaining//10)), maxfun = maxiter*25. For remaining 120s, maxiter 12? too low. Maybe `remaining//5` cap80. 120//5=24. Good. maxfun=600. Need top_pressure function:
```
def top_pressure(a,b,K,beta):
    n=a.size; L=2*n-1; K=min(int(K), L)
    if K >= L:
       idx=np.arange(L)
       vals=b
    else:
       idx=np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
       vals=b[idx]
    vmax=vals.max()
    e=np.exp(beta*(vals-vmax))
    es=e.sum()
    if es <=0 or not np.isfinite(es):
       w=np.full(K,1.0/K)
       # use top K sorted? not necessary
    else:
       w=e/es
    pressure=np.zeros(n)
    # To maybe improve, sort by vals descending for slightly? not needed but for w.
    for j in range(K):
       wj=w[j]
       if wj <= 1e-18: continue
       k=int(idx[j])
       s=max(0, k-n+1); epos=min(n-1,k)
       if s>epos: continue
       start=k-s
       end_excl=k-epos-1
       if end_excl < 0:
           part=a[start::-1]
       else:
           part=a[start:end_excl:-1]
       if part.size != epos-s+1:
           # fallback
           pos=np.arange(s, epos+1)
           part=a[k-pos]
       pressure[s:epos+1] += wj * part
    return pressure
```
Potential issue: `idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]` returns unsorted; weights correspond. If K >= L, idx arange; weights full softmax. For L=2n-1, if K_end 240, fine. For small n, K could equal L; full pressure O(n^2) if K=L; but for small n okay? For n=256, L=511, K_end maybe min240? Actually K_end max(16,n//2)=128, less. For n=64, K_end=32, L=127. Good. So not full for small? If n very small <32, K_start min. okay.

Now, `active_lp_loop` needs `solve_lp_direction`. Let's implement robust.
```
def solve_lp_direction(a,b,active):
   n=a.size; K=len(active)
   if K==0: return None
   row_chunks=[]; col_chunks=[]; data_chunks=[]
   # use int32 for indices maybe memory
   for r in range(K):
      k=int(active[r])
      s=max(0, k-n+1); e=min(n-1,k)
      start=k-s; end_excl=k-e-1
      if end_excl < 0: part=a[start::-1]
      else: part=a[start:end_excl:-1]
      Lc=e-s+1
      if Lc <=0:
         # still t col
      else:
         row_chunks.append(np.full(Lc, r, dtype=np.int32))
         col_chunks.append(np.arange(s, e+1, dtype=np.int32))
         data_chunks.append(2.0*part)
      row_chunks.append(np.array([r], dtype=np.int32))
      col_chunks.append(np.array([n], dtype=np.int32))
      data_chunks.append(np.array([-1.0], dtype=np.float64))
   if not data_chunks: return None
   A=sp.csr_matrix((np.concatenate(data_chunks), (np.concatenate(row_chunks), np.concatenate(col_chunks))), shape=(K,n+1))
   b_ub = 2.0 * np.asarray(b[active], dtype=np.float64)
   A_eq = sp.csr_matrix(([1.0], (n, np.arange(n,dtype=np.int32))), shape=(1,n+1))
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1.0
   bounds=[(0.0, None)]*n + [(-1.0, 10.0)]
   try:
      res=sciopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=[1.0], bounds=bounds, method='highs', options={'presolve': True})
   except Exception:
      try:
         res=sciopt.linprog(c, A_ub=A.toarray(), b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq.toarray(), b_eq=[1.0], bounds=bounds, method='interior-point')
      except Exception:
         return None
   if res is None or not res.success:
      return None
   g=np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
   g=np.maximum(g,0.0)
   gs=g.sum()
   if gs < 1e-15: return None
   return g/gs
```
Label toarray fallback maybe memory; but if dense too large, may fail. Fine.

`linprog` b_eq list needs float array maybe `[1.0]`, okay.

Line search in LP loop:
```
def lp_line_search(a,g,b_cur,cur_m):
    best_trial=a; best_m=cur_m; best_alpha=0
    alphas=[0.05,0.1,0.2,0.35,0.55,0.8,1.0]
    # maybe include 0.15,0.45 if not
    for al in alphas:
       trial=(1-al)*a + al*g
       trial=np.maximum(trial,0)
       s=trial.sum()
       if s<1e-300: continue
       trial=trial/s
       m=fast_autocorr(trial).max()
       if m < best_m:
          best_m=m; best_trial=trial; best_alpha=al
    return best_trial,best_m,best_alpha
```
If no improvement, best_m=cur_m.

def active_lp_loop(a0,time_limit,K=150,verbose=True):
   a=norm_sum1(a0); b=fast_autocorr(a); cur_m=b.max(); best_a=a.copy(); best_m=cur_m
   t0=time.monotonic(); it=0; no=0; rng=np.random.default_rng(99)
   max_iters=500
   while time.monotonic()-t0 < time_limit and it < max_iters:
       it+=1
       L=2*a.size-1
       Kuse=min(K,L)
       active=np.argpartition(b,-Kuse)[-Kuse:]
       active=active[np.argsort(b[active])[::-1]]
       g=solve_lp_direction(a,b,active)
       if g is None:
          # perturb
          ...; continue
       trial,m_new,alpha=lp_line_search(a,g,b,cur_m)
       if alpha > 0 and m_new < cur_m:
          a=trial; b=fast_autocorr(a); cur_m=b.max()
          no=0
          if cur_m < best_m:
             best_m=cur_m; best_a=a.copy()
       else:
          no+=1
          if no % 20 ==0:
             noise=0.002+0.01*min(3,no/50)
             a=best_a*np.exp(rng.normal(0,noise,a.size)); a=norm_sum1(a); b=fast_autocorr(a); cur_m=b.max()
             no=0
       if verbose and (it%20==0 or ...): print score
   return best_a,best_m
```
If line search returns m_new equal cur_m but alpha>0? We require <. Good.
If g has same as a, no improve.

def coarse_search_init(parent, target_n, time_limit, rng, verbose=True):
    if time_limit <=0 or target_n<=0: return None
    m=192
    starts=[]
    if parent is not None:
       starts.append(interpolate_sequence(parent,m))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,m)
    for alpha in [0.3,0.7,1.2,2.0]:
       for beta in [0.3,0.7,1.2]:
          starts.append(norm_sum1((x+0.005)**alpha * (1-x+0.005)**beta))
    for _ in range(6):
       sigma=rng.uniform(0.4,1.8)
       starts.append(norm_sum1(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,m))))
    best=None; best_m=inf
    t0=time.monotonic()
    for a in starts:
       if time.monotonic()-t0 > time_limit: break
       a_opt,m_opt=multiplicative_flatten(a, min(1.0, (time_limit-(time.monotonic()-t0))/max(1,len(starts)*0.3)), beta_start=5,beta_end=200,K_start=16,K_end=min(64,m), verbose=False)
       if m_opt < best_m:
          best_m=m_opt; best=a_opt
    if best is None: return None
    a_target=interpolate_sequence(best,target_n)
    return a_target
```

Need `time_limit // len(starts)` ~1s. If time_limit 30, 1s each. Good.

```
def propose_candidate():
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + 1740.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(123456)
    best_seq=None; best_score=float('inf')
    # load parent
    parent_arr=None
    try:
       raw=np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
       if raw.ndim !=1: raw=raw.ravel()
       raw=np.nan_to_num(raw, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
       raw=np.clip(raw,0.0,1000.0)
       if raw.size >0 and np.isfinite(raw).all() and raw.sum() > 1e-8:
           parent_arr=raw.copy()
    except Exception:
       parent_arr=None
    # candidate building
    candidates=[]
    target_lengths=[]
    if parent_arr is not None:
       pn=len(parent_arr)
       parent_norm=norm_sum1(parent_arr)
       candidates.append(('parent', parent_norm))
       best_seq,best_score=register_candidate(parent_norm,'parent',best_seq,best_score,True)
       for nl in [8192, 9000, 10000, 12000, 7500, 7000, 6500, 6000]:
           if abs(nl-pn)>100 and 3000 <= nl <= 12000:
               candidates.append(('i'+str(nl), interpolate_sequence(parent_arr,nl)))
    else:
       for nl in [8192, 10000, 7000]:
           x=np.linspace(0,1,nl)
           a=norm_sum1((x+0.01)**1.0*(1.0-x+0.01)**1.0)
           candidates.append(('def'+str(nl), a))
    # coarse
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 80:
       tcoarse=min(35.0, rem*0.02)
       coach_target = 10000 if parent_arr is None or len(parent_arr) < 10000 else min(10000, len(parent_arr))
       # maybe target 8192? Let's choose 8192/10000 depending.
       coarse = coarse_search_init(parent_arr, 8192, tcoarse, rng, verbose=False)
       if coarse is not None:
           candidates.append(('coarse8192', coarse))
       coarse2 = coarse_search_init(parent_arr, 10000, tcoarse*0.8, rng, verbose=False) if rem>150 else None
       if coarse2 is not None: candidates.append(('coarse10000', coarse2))
```

Let's write `register_candidate` with verbose prints. It uses `fast_autocorr` and `evaluate_safe`. If exact `sc` is inf due to zero sum? norm sum1 prevents. Return.

```
def register_candidate(seq, label, best_seq, best_score, force=False):
    if seq is None:
        return best_seq, best_score
    seq = norm_sum1(seq)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score
    n = seq.size
    b = fast_autocorr(seq)
    m = float(b.max())
    sc_int = 2.0 * n * m
    do_exact = force or best_seq is None or n <= 10000 or sc_int < best_score - 1e-6
    if do_exact:
        sc = evaluate_safe(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                print("  *** NEW BEST [%s]: %.10f (n=%d)" % (label, sc, n))
                seq = seq.copy()
                best_seq = seq
                best_score = float(sc)
            else:
                print("  candidate [%s]: exact=%.8f internal=%.8f best=%.8f" % (label, sc, sc_int, best_score))
        else:
            print("  candidate [%s]: invalid exact" % label)
    else:
        print("  candidate [%s]: internal=%.8f best=%.8f skipped exact" % (label, sc_int, best_score))
    return best_seq, best_score
```
If best_score inf, `sc_int < inf` true anyway. Good.

One subtlety: If best_score is not exact because we skipped large and best_seq is None? best_seq not None. Fine.

Now, in final force register, if current best exact but sc_int maybe slightly lower due FFT and sc exact same? It prints exact. Good.

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing

    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

Method:
  * normalize sequences to sum 1 (objective is scale-invariant);
  * fast FFT evaluation of convolution;
  * a multiplicative centered pressure-update loop driven by top
    active convolution peaks with an exponential-history active set;
  * active-set Frank-Wolfe LP directions by scipy HiGHS;
  * occasional L-BFGS smoothing on a log-sum-exp surrogate in log-softmax
    coordinates;
  * multi-length and coarse-to-fine探索 for global escape.

The function proposed_candidate() is the entry point and returns the best
list found before the internal time deadline.
"""

import time
import numpy as np
import scipy.optimize as sciopt
import scipy.sparse as sp

OBJ_SCALE = 1.0e8


def norm_sum1(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1:
        a = a.ravel()
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        n = max(1, a.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return a / s


def fast_autocorr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    size = 2 * n - 1
    N = 1 << (size - 1).bit_length()
    F = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    b = np.fft.irfft(F * F, N)[:size]
    b = np.where(np.isfinite(b), b, 0.0)
    return b


def evaluate_safe(a):
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
        arr = np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
        lst = [float(x) for x in arr]
        return float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
    except Exception:
        return float("inf")


def interpolate_sequence(f, new_n):
    f = norm_sum1(f)
    old_n = f.size
    if old_n == 0:
        return np.ones(max(1, int(new_n)), dtype=np.float64) / max(1, int(new_n))
    if old_n == int(new_n):
        return f.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(new_n))
    g = np.interp(x_new, x_old, f)
    return norm_sum1(g)


def softmax_logits(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64)
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-300:
        n = max(1, z.size)
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    return e / s


def full_valid_corr(p, a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    L = p.size
    need = 2 * n - 1
    if L < need:
        p = np.pad(p, (0, need - L), mode="constant")
        L = need
    conv_len = L + n - 1
    N = 1 << (conv_len - 1).bit_length()
    Ar = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    P = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(Ar * P, N)
    return np.where(np.isfinite(conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1]), conv[n - 1:2 * n - 1], 0.0)


def lse_objective(z, beta):
    a = softmax_logits(z)
    b = fast_autocorr(a)
    M = float(b.max())
    if not np.isfinite(M):
        n = z.size
        return 1e30 * OBJ_SCALE, np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    ex = np.exp(float(beta) * (b - M))
    s = float(ex.sum())
    if (not np.isfinite(s)) or s < 1e-300:
        p = np.zeros_like(b)
        k = min(50, b.size)
        idx = np.argpartition(b, -k)[-k:]
        p[idx] = 1.0 / float(k)
        lse_val = M
    else:
        p = ex / s
        lse_val = M + np.log(s) / float(beta)

    grad_a = 2.0 * full_valid_corr(p, a)
    mean = float(np.dot(a, grad_a))
    grad_z = a * (grad_a - mean)
    grad_z = np.where(np.isfinite(grad_z), grad_z, 0.0)

    f = float(lse_val) * OBJ_SCALE
    if not np.isfinite(f):
        f = M * OBJ_SCALE
    return f, grad_z * OBJ_SCALE


def smooth_lbfgs(a0, time_limit, beta_stages=(50.0, 200.0, 800.0), verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    b = fast_autocorr(a)
    best_m = float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    cur_m = best_m
    t0 = time.monotonic()

    for beta in beta_stages:
        elapsed = time.monotonic() - t0
        if elapsed > time_limit or a.size == 0:
            break
        remaining = time_limit - elapsed
        if remaining < 25.0:
            break

        z = np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-12))
        z = z - float(z.mean())
        maxiter = int(min(70, max(8, remaining / 8.0)))
        maxfun = max(150, maxiter * 18)

        try:
            res = sciopt.minimize(
                lse_objective,
                z,
                args=(float(beta),),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                jac=True,
                options={
                    "maxiter": maxiter,
                    "maxfun": maxfun,
                    "maxls": 20,
                    "disp": False,
                    "maxcor": 10,
                },
            )
        except Exception:
            continue

        if res is not None and res.x is not None:
            a_new = softmax_logits(res.x)
            b_new = fast_autocorr(a_new)
            m_new = float(b_new.max())
            if np.isfinite(m_new) and m_new < best_m:
                best_m = m_new
                best_a = a_new.copy()
            if np.isfinite(m_new) and m_new < cur_m:
                a = a_new
                cur_m = m_new
            else:
                a = best_a.copy()
                cur_m = best_m
        else:
            a = best_a.copy()
            cur_m = best_m

        if verbose:
            print("  LBFGS beta=%g  score=%.9f" % (beta, 2.0 * len(best_a) * best_m))

    return best_a, best_m


def solve_lp_direction(a, b, active):
    n = a.size
    K = active.size
    if K == 0:
        return None

    row_chunks = []
    col_chunks = []
    data_chunks = []

    for r in range(K):
        k = int(active[r])
        s = max(0, k - n + 1)
        e = min(n - 1, k)
        Lc = max(0, e - s + 1)

        if Lc > 0:
            start = k - s
            end_excl = k - e - 1
            if end_excl < 0:
                part = a[start::-1]
            else:
                part = a[start:end_excl:-1]
            if part.size != Lc:
                part = a[k - np.arange(s, e + 1)]
            row_chunks.append(np.full(Lc, r, dtype=np.int32))
            col_chunks.append(np.arange(s, e + 1, dtype=np.int32))
            data_chunks.append(2.0 * part)

        row_chunks.append(np.array([r], dtype=np.int32))
        col_chunks.append(np.array([n], dtype=np.int32))
        data_chunks.append(np.array([-1.0], dtype=np.float64))

    if not data_chunks:
        return None

    A_ub = sp.csr_matrix(
        (
            np.concatenate(data_chunks),
            (np.concatenate(row_chunks), np.concatenate(col_chunks)),
        ),
        shape=(K, n + 1),
    )
    b_ub = 2.0 * np.asarray(b[active], dtype=np.float64)
    A_eq = sp.csr_matrix(
        (np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64), (np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int32), np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32))),
        shape=(1, n + 1),
    )
    c = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    c[n] = 1.0
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(-1.0, 10.0)]

    try:
        res = sciopt.linprog(
            c,
            A_ub=A_ub,
            b_ub=b_ub,
            A_eq=A_eq,
            b_eq=np.array([1.0]),
            bounds=bounds,
            method="highs",
            options={"presolve": True},
        )
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = sciopt.linprog(
                c,
                A_ub=A_ub.toarray(),
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq.toarray(),
                b_eq=np.array([1.0]),
                bounds=bounds,
                method="interior-point",
            )
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or not res.success:
        return None

    g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if s < 1e-15:
        return None
    return g / s


def lp_line_search(a, g, b, cur_m):
    best_trial = a.copy()
    best_m = cur_m
    best_alpha = 0.0
    alphas = (0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.35, 0.55, 0.8, 1.0)

    for al in alphas:
        trial = (1.0 - al) * a + al * g
        trial = np.where(np.isfinite(trial), trial, 0.0)
        trial = np.maximum(trial, 0.0)
        s = float(trial.sum())
        if s < 1e-300:
            continue
        trial = trial / s
        m_trial = float(fast_autocorr(trial).max())
        if np.isfinite(m_trial) and m_trial < best_m:
            best_m = m_trial
            best_trial = trial
            best_alpha = al

    return best_trial, best_m, best_alpha


def active_lp_loop(a0, time_limit, K=150, verbose=True):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    b = fast_autocorr(a)
    cur_m = float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_m = cur_m
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(314159)
    no = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = t0
    L = 2 * n - 1

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit and it < 800:
        it += 1
        Kuse = min(int(K), L)
        if Kuse >= L:
            active = np.arange(L)
        else:
            active = np.argpartition(b, -Kuse)[-Kuse:]
            active = active[np.argsort(b[active])[::-1]]

        g = solve_lp_direction(a, b, active)
        if g is None:
            no += 1
            if no > 12:
                noise = 0.004 + 0.015 * min(3.0, float(no) / 40.0)
                a = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
                a = norm_sum1(a)
                b = fast_autocorr(a)
                cur_m = float(b.max())
                no = 0
            if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 10.0:
                print("  LP t=%.1f  score=%.9f  LP-fail=%d" % (time.monotonic() - t0, 2.0 * n * best_m, no))
                last_print = time.monotonic()
            continue

        trial, m_new, alpha = lp_line_search(a, g, b, cur_m)
        if alpha > 0 and m_new < cur_m:
            a = trial
            b = fast_autocorr(a)
            cur_m = float(b.max())
            no = 0
            if cur_m < best_m:
                best_m = cur_m
                best_a = a.copy()
        else:
            no += 1
            if no % 25 == 0:
                noise = 0.003 + 0.012 * min(3.0, float(no) / 60.0)
                a = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
                a = norm_sum1(a)
                b = fast_autocorr(a)
                cur_m = float(b.max())
                no = 0

        if verbose and (it % 20 == 0 or time.monotonic() - last_print > 10.0):
            print("  LP t=%.1f  it=%d  score=%.9f" % (time.monotonic() - t0, it, 2.0 * n * best_m))
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best_a, best_m


def multiplicative_flatten(
    a0,
    time_limit,
    beta_start=20.0,
    beta_end=1500.0,
    K_start=32,
    K_end=320,
    verbose=True,
):
    a = norm_sum1(a0)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a, float("inf")

    b = fast_autocorr(a)
    best_m = float(b.max())
    best_a = a.copy()
    cur_m = best_m
    L = 2 * n - 1

    if time_limit <= 0.05:
        return best_a, best_m

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    rng = np.random.default_rng(73317)

    K_end = int(min(K_end, L, max(32, n // 4)))
    K_start = int(min(K_start, K_end, max(8, n // 20)))
    if K_start > K_end:
        K_start = max(1, K_end // 2)

    beta = float(beta_start)
    eta = 0.10
    no = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = t0
    W = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
    hist_decay = 0.93

    while time.monotonic() - t0 < time_limit:
        it += 1

        progress = min(1.0, float(it) / 2000.0)
        K = int(K_start + (K_end - K_start) * progress)
        K = max(1, min(K, L))

        if K >= L:
            idx = np.arange(L)
            vals = b
        else:
            idx = np.argpartition(b, -K)[-K:]
            vals = b[idx]

        vmax = float(vals.max())
        if not np.isfinite(vmax):
            vmax = 0.0
        ex = np.exp(float(beta) * (vals - vmax))
        es = float(ex.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(es)) or es < 1e-300:
            e = np.full(K, 1.0 / float(K), dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            e = ex / es

        w_use = e + 0.5 * W[idx]
        ws = float(w_use.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(ws)) or ws < 1e-300:
            w_use = e.copy()
        else:
            w_use = w_use / ws

        W *= hist_decay
        add = 1.0 - hist_decay

        pressure = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for j in range(K):
            k = int(idx[j])
            W[k] += add * float(e[j])
            wj = float(w_use[j])
            if wj <= 1e-18:
                continue

            s = max(0, k - n + 1)
            epos = min(n - 1, k)
            if s > epos:
                continue
            start = k - s
            end_excl = k - epos - 1
            if end_excl < 0:
                part = a[start::-1]
            else:
                part = a[start:end_excl:-1]
            if part.size != epos - s + 1:
                part = a[k - np.arange(s, epos + 1)]
            pressure[s:epos + 1] += wj * part

        pressure = np.where(np.isfinite(pressure), pressure, 0.0)
        pmin = float(pressure.min())
        pmax = float(pressure.max())
        if (not np.isfinite(pmax)) or (not np.isfinite(pmin)) or (pmax - pmin) < 1e-25:
            noise = 0.004 + 0.01 * min(3.0, float(no) / 30.0)
            a = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            a = norm_sum1(a)
            b = fast_autocorr(a)
            cur_m = float(b.max())
            no = 0
            continue

        q25, q75 = np.percentile(pressure, [25.0, 75.0])
        med = 0.5 * (q25 + q75)
        scale = max(float(q75 - q25), 1e-25)
        z = (pressure - med) / scale
        z = np.tanh(z)
        z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)

        if no > 10 and (no % 5 == 0):
            z = z + rng.normal(0.0, 0.002 * min(5.0, float(no) / 30.0), n)

        logf = -eta * z
        logf = np.clip(logf, -8.0, 8.0)
        a_new = a * np.exp(logf)
        snew = float(a_new.sum())
        if (not np.isfinite(snew)) or snew < 1e-300:
            a_new = best_a.copy()
            b_new = b
            m_new = best_m
        else:
            a_new = a_new / snew
            b_new = fast_autocorr(a_new)
            m_new = float(b_new.max())

        accepted = False
        if np.isfinite(m_new) and m_new <= best_m:
            best_m = m_new
            best_a = a_new.copy()
            a = a_new
            b = b_new
            cur_m = m_new
            no = 0
            eta = min(1.25, eta * 1.15)
            accepted = True
        elif np.isfinite(m_new) and m_new <= cur_m:
            a = a_new
            b = b_new
            cur_m = m_new
            no = 0
            eta = min(1.25, eta * 1.05)
            accepted = True
        else:
            den = max(best_m, 1e-300)
            pen = (m_new - best_m) / den if np.isfinite(m_new) else 1.0
            if no > 15 and pen < max(5e-6, 2e-4 * np.exp(-float(no) / 40.0)):
                a = a_new
                b = b_new
                cur_m = m_new
                no = 0
                eta = max(0.005, eta * 0.9)
                accepted = True

            if not accepted:
                if no > 4 and (no % 5 == 0):
                    logf_s = -0.25 * eta * z
                    logf_s = np.clip(logf_s, -8.0, 8.0)
                    a_s = a * np.exp(logf_s)
                    ss = float(a_s.sum())
                    if np.isfinite(ss) and ss > 1e-300:
                        a_s = a_s / ss
                        b_s = fast_autocorr(a_s)
                        m_s = float(b_s.max())
                        if np.isfinite(m_s) and m_s <= cur_m:
                            a = a_s
                            b = b_s
                            cur_m = m_s
                            no = 0
                            eta = max(0.003, eta * 0.5)
                            accepted = True

                if not accepted:
                    no += 1
                    eta = max(0.003, eta * 0.78)
                    if no > 80:
                        noise = 0.005 + 0.02 * min(3.0, float(no) / 100.0)
                        a = best_a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
                        a = norm_sum1(a)
                        b = fast_autocorr(a)
                        cur_m = float(b.max())
                        eta = 0.08
                        no = 0

        if it % 20 == 0:
            frac = max(0.0, min(1.0, (float(it) - 50.0) / 5000.0))
            beta = float(beta_start) + (float(beta_end) - float(beta_start)) * frac
            if no > 50:
                beta = max(float(beta_start), beta * 0.9)

        if verbose and (it % 2000 == 0 or time.monotonic() - last_print > 10.0):
            print(
                "  MUL t=%6.1f  it=%6d  beta=%7.1f  eta=%.4f  score=%.9f"
                % (time.monotonic() - t0, it, beta, eta, 2.0 * n * best_m)
            )
            last_print = time.monotonic()

    return best_a, best_m


def coarse_search_init(parent, target_n, time_limit, rng, verbose=False):
    if time_limit <= 0.1 or target_n <= 0:
        return None

    m = 192
    starts = []
    if parent is not None:
        starts.append(interpolate_sequence(parent, m))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    for alpha in (0.4, 0.8, 1.3, 2.0):
        for beta in (0.4, 0.9, 1.4):
            starts.append(norm_sum1((x + 0.01) ** alpha * (1.0 - x + 0.01) ** beta))
    for _ in range(3):
        sig = rng.uniform(0.5, 1.8)
        starts.append(norm_sum1(np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, m))))

    t0 = time.monotonic()
    best = None
    best_m = float("inf")

    for i, a in enumerate(starts):
        if time.monotonic() - t0 > 0.95 * time_limit:
            break
        rem = 0.95 * time_limit - (time.monotonic() - t0)
        chunks_left = max(1, len(starts) - i)
        tl = min(2.5, rem / float(chunks_left))
        if tl <= 0.05:
            break
        a_out, m_out = multiplicative_flatten(
            a,
            tl,
            beta_start=10.0,
            beta_end=300.0,
            K_start=16,
            K_end=72,
            verbose=False,
        )
        if np.isfinite(m_out) and m_out < best_m:
            best_m = m_out
            best = a_out

    if best is None:
        return None
    return interpolate_sequence(best, int(target_n))


def register_candidate(seq, label, best_seq, best_score, force=False):
    if seq is None:
        return best_seq, best_score

    seq = norm_sum1(seq)
    if seq.size == 0:
        return best_seq, best_score

    n = seq.size
    b = fast_autocorr(seq)
    m = float(b.max())
    sc_int = 2.0 * float(n) * m

    do_exact = force or best_seq is None or n <= 9000 or sc_int < best_score - 1e-5
    if do_exact:
        sc = evaluate_safe(seq)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
                print("  *** NEW BEST [%s]: %.10f (n=%d)" % (label, sc, n))
                seq = seq.copy()
                best_seq = seq
                best_score = float(sc)
            else:
                print("  candidate [%s]: exact=%.9f internal=%.9f best=%.9f" % (label, sc, sc_int, best_score))
        else:
            print("  candidate [%s]: exact invalid" % label)
    else:
        print("  candidate [%s]: internal=%.9f best=%.9f skipped exact" % (label, sc_int, best_score))

    return best_seq, best_score


def propose_candidate():
    t_start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t_start + 1740.0
    rng = np.random.default_rng(987654321)

    best_seq = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    parent_arr = None
    try:
        raw = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
        if raw.ndim != 1:
            raw = raw.ravel()
        raw = np.where(np.isfinite(raw), raw, 0.0)
        raw = np.clip(raw, 0.0, 1000.0)
        if raw.size > 0 and raw.sum() > 1e-8:
            parent_arr = raw.copy()
    except Exception:
        parent_arr = None

    candidates = []

    if parent_arr is not None:
        pn = len(parent_arr)
        parent_norm = norm_sum1(parent_arr)
        candidates.append(("parent", parent_norm))
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(parent_norm, "parent", best_seq, best_score, True)

        for nl in (8192, 9000, 10000, 12000, 7500, 7000, 6500, 6000):
            if 3000 <= nl <= 12000 and abs(nl - pn) > 100:
                candidates.append(("i" + str(nl), interpolate_sequence(parent_arr, nl)))
    else:
        for nl in (8192, 10000, 7000):
            x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, nl)
            a = norm_sum1((x + 0.02) ** 1.0 * (1.0 - x + 0.02) ** 1.0)
            candidates.append(("default" + str(nl), a))

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 70.0:
        tc = min(35.0, rem * 0.025)
        coarse_tgt = 8192 if (parent_arr is None or len(parent_arr) < 8192) else 10000
        coarse = coarse_search_init(parent_arr, coarse_tgt, tc, rng, verbose=False)
        if coarse is not None:
            candidates.append(("coarse" + str(coarse_tgt), coarse))

    if not candidates:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 7000)
        a = norm_sum1((x + 0.02) ** 1.0 * (1.0 - x + 0.02) ** 1.0)
        candidates.append(("fallback_init", a))

    scored = []
    for i, (label, a) in enumerate(candidates):
        a = norm_sum1(a)
        m = float(fast_autocorr(a).max())
        sc = 2.0 * len(a) * m
        scored.append((sc, i, label, a))
    scored.sort()

    for sc, idx, label, a in scored[:3]:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 80.0:
            break
        tl = min(120.0, rem * 0.12)
        a_out, _ = multiplicative_flatten(
            a,
            tl,
            beta_start=20.0,
            beta_end=1200.0,
            K_start=32,
            K_end=320,
            verbose=True,
        )
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a_out, "round1_" + label, best_seq, best_score, False)

    if best_seq is None:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 7000)
        a = norm_sum1((x + 0.02) ** 1.0 * (1.0 - x + 0.02) ** 1.0)
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a, "fallback", None, float("inf"), True)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 200.0 and best_seq is not None:
        tl = min(550.0, rem * 0.38)
        a_out, _ = multiplicative_flatten(
            best_seq,
            tl,
            beta_start=50.0,
            beta_end=2200.0,
            K_start=32,
            K_end=330,
            verbose=True,
        )
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a_out, "main_mul", best_seq, best_score, False)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 170.0 and best_seq is not None:
        base_n = len(best_seq)
        ml_list = []
        for nl in (8192, 9000, 10000, 12000, 7500, 7000, 6500, 6000):
            if nl == base_n or abs(nl - base_n) > 4000 or not (3000 <= nl <= 12000):
                continue
            ac = interpolate_sequence(best_seq, nl)
            m = float(fast_autocorr(ac).max())
            ml_list.append((2.0 * nl * m, nl, ac))
        ml_list.sort()
        for sc, nl, ac in ml_list[:2]:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 90.0:
                break
            tl = min(95.0, rem * 0.13)
            a_out, _ = multiplicative_flatten(
                ac,
                tl,
                beta_start=30.0,
                beta_end=1500.0,
                K_start=32,
                K_end=330,
                verbose=True,
            )
            best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a_out, "multi_len_" + str(nl), best_seq, best_score, False)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0 and best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) <= 10000:
        tl = min(150.0, rem * 0.10)
        Klp = 110 if len(best_seq) > 8000 else 140
        a_out, _ = active_lp_loop(best_seq, tl, K=Klp, verbose=True)
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a_out, "lp_mid", best_seq, best_score, False)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180.0 and best_seq is not None:
        tl = min(330.0, rem * 0.27)
        a_out, _ = multiplicative_flatten(
            best_seq,
            tl,
            beta_start=100.0,
            beta_end=4000.0,
            K_start=48,
            K_end=360,
            verbose=True,
        )
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a_out, "final_mul1", best_seq, best_score, False)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 120.0 and best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) <= 10000:
        tl = min(110.0, rem * 0.12)
        a_out, _ = smooth_lbfgs(best_seq, tl, beta_stages=(60.0, 250.0, 900.0), verbose=True)
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a_out, "lbfgs", best_seq, best_score, False)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 55.0 and best_seq is not None:
        tl = min(75.0, rem * 0.25)
        a_out, _ = multiplicative_flatten(
            best_seq,
            tl,
            beta_start=200.0,
            beta_end=6000.0,
            K_start=64,
            K_end=400,
            verbose=False,
        )
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a_out, "final_mul2", best_seq, best_score, False)

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 35.0 and best_seq is not None and len(best_seq) <= 10000:
        tl = min(60.0, rem * 0.5)
        a_out, _ = active_lp_loop(best_seq, tl, K=90, verbose=False)
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a_out, "lp_final", best_seq, best_score, False)

    if best_seq is None:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 7000)
        a = norm_sum1((x + 0.02) ** 1.0 * (1.0 - x + 0.02) ** 1.0)
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(a, "last_fallback", None, float("inf"), True)
    else:
        best_seq, best_score = register_candidate(best_seq, "final_force", best_seq, best_score, True)

    final_sc = evaluate_safe(best_seq)
    if not np.isfinite(final_sc):
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 7000)
        a = norm_sum1((x + 0.02) ** 1.0 * (1.0 - x + 0.02) ** 1.0)
        best_seq = a
    else:
        best_score = final_sc

    out = [float(x) for x in np.asarray(norm_sum1(best_seq), dtype=np.float64)]
    return out
```
